- gen_4: 16:40 → 08:40 UTC
- gen_5: 17:49 → 09:49 UTC


- gen_6: 18:56 → 10:56 UTC
- gen_7: 19:58 → 11:58 UTC
- gen_8: 20:16 → 12:16 UTC (currently)

So a075 is actually at gen 8/10, not gen 2/10 as the registry shows—the registry metrics are stale from 06:40 UTC. The experiment is progressing well with each generation taking roughly an hour, so it should wrap up in about 2 more hours with an ETA around 14:16 UTC. But the Agent hasn't updated the registry yet, which is why there's this discrepancy.

For gen_wiki_gpt2m_a100_s42_v3, it started at 12:07 UTC and is currently in Generation 0, generating synthetic baseline data with GPT-2-medium. The output directory only has the config file so far, and the logs show it's been running for about 10 minutes with reasonable GPU utilization and memory usage for the model size, so this early stage seems normal.

Now I'm trying to understand what the "analysis_cross_alpha_prep" worker actually did. It appears to have cleaned up the killed plan_006_s42_resume job, then launched gen_wiki_gpt2m_a100_s42_v3 on the freed GPU after the previous versions failed or were terminated. The worker itself is marked as done, but I'm realizing I don't have a registry entry for it since it's a worker process rather than an experiment, so I need to figure out what triggered it and what preparation work it performed.

Looking at the current status: GPU allocation is compliant with both cuda:0 and cuda:1 in the whitelist, alpha_sweep_a075_s42 is progressing well at generation 8 out of 10 with about two hours remaining, but gen_wiki_gpt2m_a100_s42_v3 just started and needs close monitoring. There's also a discrepancy where the registry metrics for a075 are showing stale data—it reports generation 2 out of 10 when it's actually at generation 8.

Now checking the second experiment more carefully: gen_wiki_gpt2m_a100_s42_v3 is running with offline transformers mode and successfully loaded gpt2-medium from cache. Going through the spec, most parameters check out—command, server (westd-19828), GPU (cuda:0), process ID, and log path are all present—but I need to verify the data path at data/wikitext103_5k, and I notice the data count and hypothesis aren't specified in the configuration.

Looking at what's actually in the spec versus what's only in the command line, I see the seed, model name, and data path are embedded in the command string rather than as structured fields, which creates a completeness gap. Comparing this to the queued experiment gen_wiki_gpt2m_a025_s42, which does have properly populated spec fields including the hypothesis, highlights that gen_wiki_gpt2m_a100_s42_v3 is missing these structured metadata entries—this is a spec completeness issue.

Now I'm checking what files were recently modified to understand the cross-alpha analysis preparation.

_source line 106 · 2026-05-15T12:18:59.828Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
v2 failed because it used `/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-medium` as model path, but the tokenizer files (vocab.json/merges.txt) weren't there. This makes sense - the model might have been partially downloaded or the tokenizer files were missing from the local path.

v3 switched to using `gpt2-medium` (HuggingFace name) with `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1`. Since the model loaded successfully in v3 (we can see "Loading model: gpt2-medium" and "Generation 0 (baseline)" in the logs), it found the model in the HF cache. 

The offline mode confirms everything needed is cached - if any component was missing, it would've failed right there. Now I'm putting together the final review of what the worker accomplished: killed the previous run, launched the fixed version on the GPU, and generated the cross-validation output.

Looking at the findings though, there are some issues to flag. The v3 experiment is running but its registry entry is incomplete - it's missing key metadata like the hypothesis, seed, model name, and data specifications that would make it reproducible, even though the earlier queued experiment in the same series has all that information. And the cross_alpha_v5 output is essentially identical to v4 except for the version number, same file size and all four alpha points unchanged.

On the positive side, the v2 failure has been resolved by switching from a local model path to Hugging Face's cached version, which fixed the tokenizer issue and got Generation 0 running successfully. GPU allocation looks good too - both cards are being used efficiently with reasonable utilization rates.

Looking at the actual progress, the alpha_sweep job is further along than the registry indicates, already at generation 8 out of 10 with about 2 hours remaining. The wiki generation task shows lower memory utilization than expected given the GPU capacity, though this is somewhat expected for a generation phase with a moderate batch size on a smaller dataset.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: AAAI 2027 (expected deadline ~August 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings (ARR October cycle if AAAI timeline tight)\\n- Note: EMNLP 2026 Main ARR May deadline已过；AAAI 2027 给出充足实验时间\\n\\n## Narrative Structure (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n**主线**：方法论陷阱 + 多方法共识框架。Canary prediction 为系统性 case study。\\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：论文中 Claim 3 领衔 → Claim 1 (case study) → Claim 2 (regime map)。\\n**内部编号保持不变**（避免引用混乱），论文呈现时重排。\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条，内部编号不变)\\n\\n1. **Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence (论文中作为 Case Study Finding)**: token_entropy 对 diversity 退化（distinct_1/2）具有 Granger-causal precedence——领先 1-2 代，差分后仍显著，证据来自多方法一致性：cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0, |r|>0.99, p<0.004, 4/4 seed）、差分交叉相关（显著）、threshold onset（canary gen 1-2, diversity gen 3-4）、**permutation Granger（差分后仍 4/4 seed 显著，F=612-2615）**。论文可用措辞 (D025/D026 更新)：*\\\"We demonstrate Granger-causal precedence of canary metrics at α=1.0 (24/24 FDR-sig, 4 seeds). Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at intermediate α do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 在 cross-correlation 和 permutation Granger 下同样领先（差分后 4/4 SIG），但跨方法一致性弱于 token_entropy，地位为 conditional secondary canary 待 plan_006 确认（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\"信号\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\"升级为\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\"phase diagram\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\"regime map\\\"）。\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\"显著\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡——超参数选择同样致命。方法论贡献不是\\\"提供正确的检验\\\"，而是**暴露方法选择敏感性并提出多方法一致性框架**——只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。这是迭代训练文献中被忽视的方法论陷阱。\\n\\n## Story Spine (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n- **Problem**: Model collapse 文献中的 lead-lag 分析（\\\"哪些指标预测崩溃\\\"）日益增多（Du et al., SIGMA, DCES），但**方法选择对结论的影响未被系统性审视**。Du et al. 非正式观察到 ECE 领先 accuracy，仅限单指标对、1-3 代、无统计检验。现有工作的方法论基础薄弱：无差分检验（不排除伪回归）、无多方法交叉验证、无多重比较校正、无 regime-dependent 分析。\\n\\n- **Gap**: 没有工作系统性回答：(1) lead-lag 结论在多大程度上取决于分析方法的选择？(2) 同一统计检验在不同 contamination regime 下结论是否稳定？(3) 如何建立可靠的 lead-lag 判定框架？**Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012)** 在生态/气候科学中建立了临界转变早期预警框架，但尚未被 model collapse 文献引入，其方法论严谨性（多方法交叉验证 + regime-dependent analysis）恰好是本领域所缺失的。\\n\\n- **Our approach**: 以 canary prediction for model collapse 为系统性 case study，暴露 lead-lag 分析中的三个方法论陷阱：(1) 方法选择敏感性——同一数据集上 5 种统计方法从 100% 显著到 0% 显著（Claim 3）；(2) 未差分的伪回归——canary→perplexity 通道在差分后完全消失（Claim 1 case study）；(3) regime-dependent validity——同一检验在不同 α 下结论翻转（Claim 2）。在此基础上提出**多方法一致性共识框架**（multi-method consensus framework）作为解决方案：只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。\\n\\n- **Headline result (D027, Reviewer 优化版)**: \\\"In iterative synthetic training with Pythia-410M, naive correlation identifies 100% of canary-collapse metric pairs as significant, while differenced permutation Granger retains only 25% — and this method-dependent gap is compounded by regime dependence: the same test applied to the same metric pair yields 24/24 FDR-significant results at α=1.0 but 0/12 at intermediate contamination levels. We propose a multi-method consensus framework and, applying it, identify token entropy as a robust 1-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse under high contamination.\\\"\\n\\n- **Title direction**: \\\"Method Matters: Pitfalls and a Multi-Method Consensus Framework for Lead-Lag Analysis in Iterative Training Collapse\\\"\\n\\n- **文献定位 (保留)**：SIGMA 提供谱监控但无 lead-time 量化；DCES 反应式而非预测式；Du et al. 是最接近的经验前辈但方法论不足；Kalinowski 拓扑 CI 针对表征坍缩非合成数据退化且计算昂贵。Truong & Truong 证明一阶相变下标准早期预警指标失效。理论分析方面：Seddik et al. (2024), Gerstgrasser et al. (2024), Dey et al. (2024) 提供互补理论视角但不涉及方法论敏感性。**我们与 Du et al. 的核心区别**：(1) 多指标家族系统对比；(2) 多方法交叉验证 + d_max sensitivity；(3) regime-dependent mapping；(4) **方法选择敏感性本身是主要贡献**（不再是\\\"独立贡献\\\"而是\\\"核心贡献\\\"）。\\n\\n## Anticipated **effective_rank 和 self_bleu 作为 canary 失败** — 必须全量报告，不能只报正面结果\\n4. **distinct_3 必须放 supplementary，不能完全省略** — max_lag=7 下升为 SIGNIFICANT 但 lag_std>1.5（双峰：seed 42 总是 -5，其余 -1/-2），维持 D008 降级策略，主文聚焦 distinct_1/2。Supplementary 需给完整结果 + 解释 lag instability（高阶 n-gram diversity 对短序列随机变异更敏感）。distinct_3 的不稳定性本身支持 \\\"predictability boundary\\\" 主题——即使 diversity 维度内部也有可预测性梯度\\n5. **max_lag=7 确认核心 lead 无截断** — token_entropy→distinct_1 lag=-1（std=0.0）、ECE→distinct_2 lag=-1（std=0.0）在 max_lag=5/7 下完全一致。token_entropy→perplexity lag=-5 虽非截断伪影，但已确认为非平稳伪回归（D017）\\n6. **~~Dual-timescale 升级条件~~ — 已失效 (D017)** — perplexity 通道经差分确认为伪回归，即使 plan_006 T=20 也无法拯救伪信号。\\\"Two distinct lead horizons\\\" 升级路径关闭。plan_006 的价值重新定位为：(1) diversity lead 在更长序列中的稳定性，(2) TY power 改善观察，(3) 差分后 permutation Granger 在 T=20 下的表现\\n7. **ECE→perplexity \\\"反向因果\\\"已确认为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后 ECE→perplexity 0/4 SIG。之前的 post-hoc mechanistic hypothesis 无需讨论。论文只需报告差分前后对比\\n8. **TY 在 N=11 下完全不可用于区分真/伪关联** — d_max sensitivity 分析证实：d_max=0 过度宽松（连 effective_rank 都通过），d_max=1 过度保守（连 token_entropy→distinct_1 都拒绝），adaptive 因 ADF 不稳定而跨 seed 震荡。论文**不能**用 TY 作为\\\"正确答案\\\"。**spurious_rate 必须双版本报告**：含 TY 75%（16 pair 中 12 仅 TY 判伪）、排除 TY 后 62.5%（16 pair 中 10 在 4 种非 TY 方法下一致）。ECE→distinct_2/3 被 TY 判伪但在其余 4 种方法全部 4/4 SIG——这本身是\\\"方法选择改变分类\\\"的例证（Claim 3 素材）\\n9. **ECE 地位为 conditional secondary canary (D020)** — ECE 在 seed）。论文标注：\\\"secondary only in TY consistency; equivalent across all other methods\\\"。plan_006（N=21）为最终判定——若 TY 在更大 N 下确认 ECE→distinct_2/3，升级为 co-primary\\n10. **d_max sensitivity 曲线是论文的 key figure** — 三组 d_max（0/adaptive/1）× spurious rate 的变化是 Claim 3 的核心可视化，展示\\\"方法选择如何改变结论\\\"\\n11. **统计效力必须显式论证** — Dream 三档一致 CRITICAL：T=10 下 TY 的 DOF 严重不足。论文必须包含 bootstrap/permutation 验证 + plan_006 (T=20) 结果。所有 p-value 必须经 BH FDR 校正\\n12. **\\\"phase diagram\\\" → \\\"regime map\\\"** — 除非 α 分辨率达到 ≥6 个非平凡值 + 提供 α* uncertainty interval，否则用 \\\"predictability regime map\\\" 或 \\\"regime-dependent analysis\\\"。4-5 个 α 值不足以支撑物理学意义上的 \\\"phase diagram\\\"\\n13. **Scheffer et al. 是必引文献** — critical slowing down / early-warning indicators 框架是本研究的知识祖先。论文 Related Work 必须引用并定位\\n14. **CRS 降级为 illustration** — 不列入核心 contribution。CRS 是显然的反应式干预，Dream 三档一致认为 trivial/confounded\\n15. **TY 正式降级为辅助指标 (D015)** — Bootstrap power analysis (N=1000 Monte Carlo): T=10 power=0.05-0.20, T=20 power=0.17, T=30 power=0.29 (d_max≥1)。核心分析框架改为 cross-correlation + permutation test + BH FDR 校正。TY 仅用于 Claim 3 展示（方法敏感性 + power analysis figure），不再作为 lead-lag 判定方法\\n16. **Perplexity 通道确认为伪回归 (D017)** — Permutation Granger 差分版：canary→perplexity 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99)，canary→diversity 差分后 12/12 SIG（6 pair × 4 seed 中，token_entropy 和 ECE 各 3 pair 全通过）。差分前后结论翻转（perplexity: 6-8/8→0/8; diversity: 12/12→12/12）是 Claim 3 的关键 figure 素材。论文 Method 节须注明：(1) T=11 first-difference 后 T=10，circular shift 仅 9 个唯一排列，null distribution 高度离散；(2) 尽管如此，diversity 通道 F 值远超任何排列值，结论不受离散性影响\\n17. **f² 效应量报告约束 (D019 更新)** — 差分后 f²=102-435（token_entropy→distinct_1），因 df_resid=5-6 任何真实信号都产生极大 f²（RSS_unrestricted 趋近零）。论文**禁止**用 Cohen 的 0.02/0.15/0.35 基准解读这些 f² 值。正确做法：permutation p-value 为主要推断证据，f² 仅作\\\"效应存在\\\"的定性指示，不做定量比较。**代码层面**：当 df_resid < 20 时，分析脚本输出 `f2_size=\\\"df_too_low\\\"` 而非 small/medium/large 分类（D019 T3 已实现于 permutation_granger.py）\\n18. **2×2 差分对比表是 Claim 3 核心 figure** — channel type（canary→diversity vs canary→perplexity）× differencing status（undifferenced vs differenced）的 2×2 表最紧凑展示\\\"一个方法论选择翻转整条通道的结论\\\"。diversity 通道差分前后均 SIG（F 不降反升），perplexity 通道差分后归零。比 d_max sensitivity 曲线更直观地展示差分的决定性影响\\n19. **method_ablation 必须双版本报告 (D019)** — spurious_rate 报告含 TY（5-method: 75%）和排除 TY（4-method: 62.5%）两个数字。ECE→distinct_2/3 在 TY 下被判伪但在其余 4 种方法中 4/4 SIG——TY 的 Type II error 驱动的分类差异本身是 Claim 3 素材。论文主文用 4-method 数字作主要结论，5-method 数字用于 Claim 3 方法敏感性讨论\\n20. **ECE canary 可靠性是 regime-dependent (D021)** — α=0.25 下 ECE gen 1 下降 -14%（反向信号），gen 2+ 才上升至 +77%。α=1.0 下 gen 1 直接 +2926%。ECE 作为早期预警 canary 仅在高 α（≥0.50，待 a050 确认）下可靠。低 α 下首代反向信号意味着 ECE 不能用于\\\"一代内预警\\\"。这丰富 Claim 2：canary 可靠性本身是 regime-dependent。D019 \\\"co-primary\\\" 限定为 \\\"high-α regime only\\\"\\n21. **Claim 1 α 适用范围确认收窄 (D022→D025)** — a050 结果确认 scenario (b)：α=0.50 两种因果检验方法 FDR 后归零（TY 0/8，permG 0/8），三种 correlation/rule-based 方法全部通过（naive 8/8，differenced 8/8，threshold 8/8）→ 3/5 方法通过，未达 ≥4/5 共识。α=0.25 也仅 TY 3/8 FDR-sig（power 存疑）。**当前 Granger-causal precedence 仅 α=1.0 稳固。** α=0.75（QUEUED）将决定 boundary：若 α=0.75 显著 → \\\"α ≥ 0.75\\\"；若也 null → \\\"α=1.0 only\\\"。论文措辞：Claim 1 主句限定为 \\\"at high contamination levels\\\"，Claim 2 regime map 展示 detectability boundary 的确切位置。不删 \\\"Granger-causal\\\"——差分后 α=1.0 仍 24/24 FDR-sig 是货真价实的 Granger 证据\\n22. **α=0.50 null 结果是 positive finding (D025)** — 中等污染下 canary 与 downstream 同步退化（temporal lead 消失），correlation/rule-based 方法（naive, differenced, threshold）100% sig vs 因果检验方法（permG, TY）0% sig 是 Claim 3（method × regime interaction）最极端的例证。论文呈现策略：(1) 此非单调 detectability 模式本身是 regime map 的核心特征；(2) **贡献定位是映射可预测性边界，不是声称通用预警**——α=1.0（100% 合成替换）在实践中极端，practical utility argument 须等 α=0.75 结果定调：若 α=0.75 有信号则 boundary 在 0.50-0.75，实用价值显著提升；若也归零则 utility 论述须谨慎，退回纯 \\\"boundary mapping\\\" 贡献定位；(3) α=0.50 null 不是方法失败，是 signal-to-noise 的物理边界\\n23. **α-sweep 全部 single seed 是当前最大弱点 (D026)** — 非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 -40.3% vs α=0.50 -37.3%）和 ECE regime switch 假说均基于 seed=42 的 point estimate，无置信区间。论文中非单调性结论**必须标注 \\\"observed at seed=42; multi-seed confirmation required\\\"**。multi-seed（α=0.25/0.50 各 2-3 seeds）是从 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要条件，优先于 finer α grid（a010/a060）。非单调性在主文仅报告现象（配 drift 表格），\\\"entropy floor\\\" 机制假说放 Discussion\\n24. **α=0.25 双向 Granger 不改变 Claim 1 叙事 (D026)** — d1→entropy raw p=0.023（FDR 后 0.093 不显著），但效应量仅 f²=1.30 vs entropy→d1 的 f²=27.21（21 倍差异）。强单向因果 + 弱反馈，不是对称 coupled dynamics。α=1.0 反方向不显著，α=0.50 反方向 p=0.224。论文可加一句：\\\"marginally significant feedback at mild contamination (f²=1.30, 21× weaker than primary direction)\\\" 但不需要改写核心叙事\\n25. **证据强度排序：Claim 3 > Claim 2 > Claim 1 (D026)** — Reviewer 独立评估。Claim 3（method sensitivity）最强且 novel、well-supported；Claim 2（regime-dependent）次之（但需 multi-seed）；Claim 1（Granger-causal）scope 最窄（仅 α=1.0）。论文 framing 应确保 Claim 3 的方法论贡献获得足够篇幅，不因 Claim 1 的 \\\"canary prediction\\\" 叙事而被矮化\\n\", \"total_chars\": 15252, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\\\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 在 cross-correlation 和 permutation Granger 下同样领先（差分后 4/4 SIG），但跨方法一致性弱于 token_entropy，地位为 conditional secondary canary 待 plan_006 确认（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。\\\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\\\\\"信号\\\\\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\\\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\\\\\"升级为\\\\\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\\\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\\\\\"phase diagram\\\\\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\\\\\"regime map\\\\\\\"）。\\\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\\\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\\\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\\\\\"显著\\\\\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡——超参数选择同样致命。方法论贡献不是\\\\\\\"提供正确的检验\\\\\\\"，而是**暴露方法选择敏感性并提出多方法一致性框架**——只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。这是迭代训练文献中被忽视的方法论陷阱。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine (D027: Option B reframe)\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Model collapse 文献中的 lead-lag 分析（\\\\\\\"哪些指标预测崩溃\\\\\\\"）日益增多（Du et al., SIGMA, DCES），但**方法选择对结论的影响未被系统性审视**。Du et al. 非正式观察到 ECE 领先 accuracy，仅限单指标对、1-3 代、无统计检验。现有工作的方法论基础薄弱：无差分检验（不排除伪回归）、无多方法交叉验证、无多重比较校正、无 regime-dependent 分析。\\\\n\\\\n- **Gap**: 没有工作系统性回答：(1) lead-lag 结论在多大程度上取决于分析方法的选择？(2) 同一统计检验在不同 contamination regime 下结论是否稳定？(3) 如何建立可靠的 lead-lag 判定框架？**Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012)** 在生态/气候科学中建立了临界转变早期预警框架，但尚未被 model collapse 文献引入，其方法论严谨性（多方法交叉验证 + regime-dependent analysis）恰好是本领域所缺失的。\\\\n\\\\n- **Our approach**: 以 canary prediction for model collapse 为系统性 case study，暴露 lead-lag 分析中的三个方法论陷阱：(1) 方法选择敏感性——同一数据集上 5 种统计方法从 100% 显著到 0% 显著（Claim 3）；(2) 未差分的伪回归——canary→perplexity 通道在差分后完全消失（Claim 1 case study）；(3) regime-dependent validity——同一检验在不同 α 下结论翻转（Claim 2）。在此基础上提出**多方法一致性共识框架**（multi-method consensus framework）作为解决方案：只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。\\\\n\\\\n- **Headline result (D027, Reviewer 优化版)**: \\\\\\\"In iterative synthetic training with Pythia-410M, naive correlation identifies 100% of canary-collapse metric pairs as significant, while differenced permutation Granger retains only 25% — and this method-dependent gap is compounded by regime dependence: the same test applied to the same metric pair yields 24/24 FDR-significant results at α=1.0 but 0/12 at intermediate contamination levels. We propose a multi-method consensus framework and, applying it, identify token entropy as a robust 1-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse under high contamination.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n- **Title direction**: \\\\\\\"Method Matters: Pitfalls and a Multi-Method Consensus Framework for Lead-Lag Analysis in Iterative Training Collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n- **文献定位 (保留)**：SIGMA 提供谱监控但无 lead-time 量化；DCES 反应式而非预测式；Du et al. 是最接近的经验前辈但方法论不足；Kalinowski 拓扑 CI 针对表征坍缩非合成数据退化且计算昂贵。Truong & Truong 证明一阶相变下标准早期预警指标失效。理论分析方面：Seddik et al. (2024), Gerstgrasser et al. (2024), Dey et al. (2024) 提供互补理论视角但不涉及方法论敏感性。**我们与 Du et al. 的核心区别**：(1) 多指标家族系统对比；(2) 多方法交叉验证 + d_max sensitivity；(3) regime-dependent mapping；(4) **方法选择敏感性本身是主要贡献**（不再是\\\\\\\"独立贡献\\\\\\\"而是\\\\\\\"核心贡献\\\\\\\"）。\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Responses\\\\n\\\\n| Concern | Response Strategy | Required Experiment |\\\\n|---------|-------------------|---------------------|\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Canary 不预测 perplexity，实用价值有限\\\\\\\" | 差分检验已确认 canary→perplexity 为伪回归（D017）。Framing 转为\\\\\\\"canary 预测 diversity collapse\\\\\\\"——diversity 是 collapse 最早可观测维度，实用价值在于比 perplexity 更早预警。perplexity 通道的伪信号本身是方法论贡献（Claim 3 例证） | 已有数据（D017 确认） |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"ECE 和 token_entropy 对 perplexity 方向相反？\\\\\\\" | 差分后两者对 autocorrelation/variance 类比 | 文献工作 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"双变量 Granger 无法排除混淆因子\\\\\\\" | token_entropy 和 ECE 可能共享信息源（均捕捉 output distribution shape），双变量 Granger 无法排除未观测混淆因子。论文 Discussion 须显式声明 Granger precedence ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），并讨论多变量 VAR 或条件 Granger 作为 future work。多方法一致性（4 种独立方法收敛）部分缓解但不消除此顾虑 | Discussion 声明 + future work |\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit\\\\n\\\\nAAAI 2027 优势：\\\\n- ML 方法论 track 接受 empirical analysis + 统计方法论贡献\\\\n- 不要求 SOTA，接受 systematic study with novel methodology\\\\n- Regime map / predictability boundary 概念在 AAAI 偏理论的审稿人中有吸引力\\\\n\\\\n风险：\\\\n- 如果被认为\\\\\\\"只是 empirical study 没有算法贡献\\\\\\\"→ 多方法一致性框架 + 方法选择敏感性分析本身是方法论贡献（Claim 3）；CRS 降级为 illustration 不作独立贡献\\\\n- 模型规模偏小（Pythia-410M/1.4B）→ 诚实标注 limitation + scaling trend\\\\n\\\\n## Key Narrative Constraints (from MVP + plan_003)\\\\n\\\\n1. **不能 claim \\\\\\\"canary predicts model collapse\\\\\\\"** — 只能 claim \\\\\\\"canary predicts diversity collapse\\\\\\\"。token_entropy→perplexity 经差分检验确认为伪回归（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99, D017），未差分时的\\\\\\\"弱信号\\\\\\\"完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动。论文措辞用\\\\\\\"spurious association confirmed by first-differencing\\\\\\\"，不再用\\\\\\\"弱信号\\\\\\\"\\\\n2. **ECE→perplexity 和 token_entropy→perplexity 均为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后两者均 0/4 seed 显著。未差分时观察到的\\\\\\\"方向相反\\\\\\\"（token_entropy 领先 vs perplexity 领先 ECE）是共享趋势的不同表现形式。论文报告差分前后对比作为方法论教训，不再需要机制性解释\\\\n3. **effective_rank 和 self_bleu 作为 canary 失败** — 必须全量报告，不能只报正面结果\\\\n4. **distinct_3 必须放 supplementary，不能完全省略** — max_lag=7 下升为 SIGNIFICANT 但 lag_std>1.5（双峰：seed 42 总是 -5，其余 -1/-2），维持 D008 降级策略，主文聚焦 distinct_1/2。Supplementary 需给完整结果 + 解释 lag instability（高阶 n-gram diversity 对短序列随机变异更敏感）。distinct_3 的不稳定性本身支持 \\\\\\\"predictability boundary\\\\\\\" 主题——即使 diversity 维度内部也有可预测性梯度\\\\n5. **max_lag=7 确认核心 lead 无截断** — token_entropy→distinct_1 lag=-1（std=0.0）、ECE→distinct_2 lag=-1（std=0.0）在 max_lag=5/7 下完全一致。token_entropy→perplexity lag=-5 虽非截断伪影，但已确认为非平稳伪回归（D017）\\\\n6. **~~Dual-timescale 升级条件~~ — 已失效 (D017)** — perplexity 通道经差分确认为伪回归，即使 plan_006 T=20 也无法拯救伪信号。\\\\\\\"Two distinct lead horizons\\\\\\\" 升级路径关闭。plan_006 的价值重新定位为：(1) diversity lead 在更长序列中的稳定性，(2) TY power 改善观察，(3) 差分后 permutation Granger 在 T=20 下的表现\\\\n7. **ECE→perplexity \\\\\\\"反向因果\\\\\\\"已确认为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后 ECE→perplexity 0/4 SIG。之前的 post-hoc mechanistic hypothesis 无需讨论。论文只需报告差分前后对比\\\\n8. **TY 在 N=11 下完全不可用于区分真/伪关联** — d_max sensitivity 分析证实：d_max=0 过度宽松（连 effective_rank 都通过），d_max=1 过度保守（连 token_entropy→distinct_1 都拒绝），adaptive 因 ADF 不稳定而跨 seed 震荡。论文**不能**用 TY 作为\\\\\\\"正确答案\\\\\\\"。**spurious_rate 必须双版本报告**：含 TY 75%（16 pair 中 12 仅 TY 判伪）、排除 TY 后 62.5%（16 pair 中 10 在 4 种非 TY 方法下一致）。ECE→distinct_2/3 被 TY 判伪但在其余 4 种方法全部 4/4 SIG——这本身是\\\\\\\"方法选择改变分类\\\\\\\"的例证（Claim 3 素材）\\\\n9. **ECE 地位为 conditional secondary canary (D020)** — ECE 在 cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0）和 permutation Granger（差分后 4/4 SIG）下与 token_entropy 等效，ECE→distinct_2/3 在排除 TY 的 4 种方法中全部通过。\\\\\\\"secondary\\\\\\\" 标签**完全由 TY 的 Type II error 驱动**（TY 仅通过 ECE→distinct_1 3/4 seed）。论文标注：\\\\\\\"secondary only in TY consistency; equivalent across all other methods\\\\\\\"。plan_006（N=21）为最终判定——若 TY 在更大 N 下确认 ECE→distinct_2/3，升级为 co-primary\\\\n10. **d_max sensitivity 曲线是论文的 key figure** — 三组 d_max（0/adaptive/1）× spurious rate 的变化是 Claim 3 的核心可视化，展示\\\\\\\"方法选择如何改变结论\\\\\\\"\\\\n11. **统计效力必须显式论证** — Dream 三档一致 CRITICAL：T=10 下 TY 的 DOF 严重不足。论文必须包含 bootstrap/permutation 验证 + plan_006 (T=20) 结果。所有 p-value 必须经 BH FDR 校正\\\\n12. **\\\\\\\"phase diagram\\\\\\\" → \\\\\\\"regime map\\\\\\\"** — 除非 α 分辨率达到 ≥6 个非平凡值 + 提供 α* uncertainty interval，否则用 \\\\\\\"predictability regime map\\\\\\\" 或 \\\\\\\"regime-dependent analysis\\\\\\\"。4-5 个 α 值不足以支撑物理学意义上的 \\\\\\\"phase diagram\\\\\\\"\\\\n13. **Scheffer et al. 是必引文献** — critical slowing down / early-warning indicators 框架是本研究的知识祖先。论文 Related Work 必须引用并定位\\\\n14. **CRS 降级为 illustration** — 不列入核心 contribution。CRS 是显然的反应式干预，Dream 三档一致认为 trivial/confounded\\\\n15. **TY 正式降级为辅助指标 (D015)** — Bootstrap power analysis (N=1000 Monte Carlo): T=10 power=0.05-0.20, T=20 power=0.17, T=30 power=0.29 (d_max≥1)。核心分析框架改为 cross-correlation + permutation test + BH FDR 校正。TY 仅用于 Claim 3 展示（方法敏感性 + power analysis figure），不再作为 lead-lag 判定方法\\\\n16. **Perplexity 通道确认为伪回归 (D017)** — Permutation Granger 差分版：canary→perplexity 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99)，canary→diversity 差分后 12/12 SIG（6 pair × 4 seed 中，token_entropy 和 ECE 各 3 pair 全通过）。差分前后结论翻转（perplexity: 6-8/8→0/8; diversity: 12/12→12/12）是 Claim 3 的关键 figure 素材。论文 Method 节须注明：(1) T=11 first-difference 后 T=10，circular shift 仅 9 个唯一排列，null distribution 高度离散；(2) 尽管如此，diversity 通道 F 值远超任何排列值，结论不受离散性影响\\\\n17. **f² 效应量报告约束 (D019 更新)** — 差分后 f²=102-435（token_entropy→distinct_1），因 df_resid=5-6 任何真实信号都产生极大 f²（RSS_unrestricted 趋近零）。论文**禁止**用 Cohen 的 0.02/0.15/0.35 基准解读这些 f² 值。正确做法：permutation p-value 为主要推断证据，f² 仅作\\\\\\\"效应存在\\\\\\\"的定性指示，不做定量比较。**代码层面**：当 df_resid < 20 时，分析脚本输出 `f2_size=\\\\\\\"df_too_low\\\\\\\"` 而非 small/medium/large 分类（D019 T3 已实现于 permutation_granger.py）\\\\n18. **2×2 差分对比表是 Claim 3 核心 figure** — channel type（canary→diversity vs canary→perplexity）× differencing status（undifferenced vs differenced）的 2×2 表最紧凑展示\\\\\\\"一个方法论选择翻转整条通道的结论\\\\\\\"。diversity 通道差分前后均 SIG（F 不降反升），perplexity 通道差分后归零。比 d_max sensitivity 曲线更直观地展示差分的决定性影响\\\\n19. **method_ablation 必须双版本报告 (D019)** — spurious_rate 报告含 TY（5-method: 75%）和排除 TY（4-method: 62.5%）两个数字。ECE→distinct_2/3 在 TY 下被判伪但在其余 4 种方法中 4/4 SIG——TY 的 Type II error 驱动的分类差异本身是 Claim 3 素材。论文主文用 4-method 数字作主要结论，5-method 数字用于 Claim 3 方法敏感性讨论\\\\n20. **ECE canary 可靠性是 regime-dependent (D021)** — α=0.25 下 ECE gen 1 下降 -14%（反向信号），gen 2+ 才上升至 +77%。α=1.0 下 gen 1 直接 +2926%。ECE 作为早期预警 canary 仅在高 α（≥0.50，待 a050 确认）下可靠。低 α 下首代反向信号意味着 ECE 不能用于\\\\\\\"一代内预警\\\\\\\"。这丰富 Claim 2：canary 可靠性本身是 regime-dependent。D019 \\\\\\\"co-primary\\\\\\\" 限定为 \\\\\\\"high-α regime only\\\\\\\"\\\\n21. **Claim 1 α 适用范围确认收窄 (D022→D025)** — a050 结果确认 scenario (b)：α=0.50 两种因果检验方法 FDR 后归零（TY 0/8，permG 0/8），三种 correlation/rule-based 方法全部通过（naive 8/8，differenced 8/8，threshold 8/8）→ 3/5 方法通过，未达 ≥4/5 共识。α=0.25 也仅 TY 3/8 FDR-sig（power 存疑）。**当前 Granger-causal precedence 仅 α=1.0 稳固。** α=0.75（QUEUED）将决定 boundary：若 α=0.75 显著 → \\\\\\\"α ≥ 0.75\\\\\\\"；若也 null → \\\\\\\"α=1.0 only\\\\\\\"。论文措辞：Claim 1 主句限定为 \\\\\\\"at high contamination levels\\\\\\\"，Claim 2 regime map 展示 detectability boundary 的确切位置。不删 \\\\\\\"Granger-causal\\\\\\\"——差分后 α=1.0 仍 24/24 FDR-sig 是货真价实的 Granger 证据\\\\n22. **α=0.50 null 结果是 positive finding (D025)** — 中等污染下 canary 与 downstream 同步退化（temporal lead 消失），correlation/rule-based 方法（naive, differenced, threshold）100% sig vs 因果检验方法（permG, TY）0% sig 是 Claim 3（method × regime interaction）最极端的例证。论文呈现策略：(1) 此非单调 detectability 模式本身是 regime map 的核心特征；(2) **贡献定位是映射可预测性边界，不是声称通用预警**——α=1.0（100% 合成替换）在实践中极端，practical utility argument 须等 α=0.75 结果定调：若 α=0.75 有信号则 boundary 在 0.50-0.75，实用价值显著提升；若也归零则 utility 论述须谨慎，退回纯 \\\\\\\"boundary mapping\\\\\\\" 贡献定位；(3) α=0.50 null 不是方法失败，是 signal-to-noise 的物理边界\\\\n23. **α-sweep 全部 single seed 是当前最大弱点 (D026)** — 非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 -40.3% vs α=0.50 -37.3%）和 ECE regime switch 假说均基于 seed=42 的 point estimate，无置信区间。论文中非单调性结论**必须标注 \\\\\\\"observed at seed=42; multi-seed confirmation required\\\\\\\"**。multi-seed（α=0.25/0.50 各 2-3 seeds）是从 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要条件，优先于 finer α grid（a010/a060）。非单调性在主文仅报告现象（配 drift 表格），\\\\\\\"entropy floor\\\\\\\" 机制假说放 Discussion\\\\n24. **α=0.25 双向 Granger 不改变 Claim 1 叙事 (D026)** — d1→entropy raw p=0.023（FDR 后       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: AAAI 2027 (expected deadline ~August 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings (ARR October cycle if AAAI timeline tight)\\n- Note: EMNLP 2026 Main ARR May deadline已过；AAAI 2027 给出充足实验时间\\n\\n## Narrative Structure (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n**主线**：方法论陷阱 + 多方法共识框架。Canary prediction 为系统性 case study。\\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：论文中 Claim 3 领衔 → Claim 1 (case study) → Claim 2 (regime map)。\\n**内部编号保持不变**（避免引用混乱），论文呈现时重排。\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条，内部编号不变)\\n\\n1. **Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence (论文中作为 Case Study Finding)**: token_entropy 对 diversity 退化（distinct_1/2）具有 Granger-causal precedence——领先 1-2 代，差分后仍显著，证据来自多方法一致性：cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0, |r|>0.99, p<0.004, 4/4 seed）、差分交叉相关（显著）、threshold onset（canary gen 1-2, diversity gen 3-4）、**permutation Granger（差分后仍 4/4 seed 显著，F=612-2615）**。论文可用措辞 (D025/D026 更新)：*\\\"We demonstrate Granger-causal precedence of canary metrics at α=1.0 (24/24 FDR-sig, 4 seeds). Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at intermediate α do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 在 cross-correlation 和 permutation Granger 下同样领先（差分后 4/4 SIG），但跨方法一致性弱于 token_entropy，地位为 conditional secondary canary 待 plan_006 确认（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\"信号\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\"升级为\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\"phase diagram\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\"regime map\\\"）。\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\"显著\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡——超参数选择同样致命。方法论贡献不是\\\"提供正确的检验\\\"，而是**暴露方法选择敏感性并提出多方法一致性框架**——只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。这是迭代训练文献中被忽视的方法论陷阱。\\n\\n## Story Spine (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n- **Problem**: Model collapse 文献中的 lead-lag 分析（\\\"哪些指标预测崩溃\\\"）日益增多（Du et al., SIGMA, DCES），但**方法选择对结论的影响未被系统性审视**。Du et al. 非正式观察到 ECE 领先 accuracy，仅限单指标对、1-3 代、无统计检验。现有工作的方法论基础薄弱：无差分检验（不排除伪回归）、无多方法交叉验证、无多重比较校正、无 regime-dependent 分析。\\n\\n- **Gap**: 没有工作系统性回答：(1) lead-lag 结论在多大程度上取决于分析方法的选择？(2) 同一统计检验在不同 contamination regime 下结论是否稳定？(3) 如何建立可靠的 lead-lag 判定框架？**Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012)** 在生态/气候科学中建立了临界转变早期预警框架，但尚未被 model collapse 文献引入，其方法论严谨性（多方法交叉验证 + regime-dependent analysis）恰好是本领域所缺失的。\\n\\n- **Our approach**: 以 canary prediction for model collapse 为系统性 case study，暴露 lead-lag 分析中的三个方法论陷阱：(1) 方法选择敏感性——同一数据集上 5 种统计方法从 100% 显著到 0% 显著（Claim 3）；(2) 未差分的伪回归——canary→perplexity 通道在差分后完全消失（Claim 1 case study）；(3) regime-dependent validity——同一检验在不同 α 下结论翻转（Claim 2）。在此基础上提出**多方法一致性共识框架**（multi-method consensus framework）作为解决方案：只有跨方法（cross-corr + 差分 + threshold + permutation Granger）和跨 seed 都一致的关联才可信。\\n\\n- **Headline result (D027, Reviewer 优化版)**: \\\"In iterative synthetic training with Pythia-410M, naive correlation identifies 100% of canary-collapse metric pairs as significant, while differenced permutation Granger retains only 25% — and this method-dependent gap is compounded by regime dependence: the same test applied to the same metric pair yields 24/24 FDR-significant results at α=1.0 but 0/12 at intermediate contamination levels. We propose a multi-method consensus framework and, applying it, identify token entropy as a robust 1-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse under high contamination.\\\"\\n\\n- **Title direction**: \\\"Method Matters: Pitfalls and a Multi-Method Consensus Framework for Lead-Lag Analysis in Iterative Training Collapse\\\"\\n\\n- **文献定位 (保留)**：SIGMA 提供谱监控但无 lead-time 量化；DCES 反应式而非预测式；Du et al. 是最接近的经验前辈但方法论不足；Kalinowski 拓扑 CI 针对表征坍缩非合成数据退化且计算昂贵。Truong & Truong 证明一阶相变下标准早期预警指标失效。理论分析方面：Seddik et al. (2024), Gerstgrasser et al. (2024), Dey et al. (2024) 提供互补理论视角但不涉及方法论敏感性。**我们与 Du et al. 的核心区别**：(1) 多指标家族系统对比；(2) 多方法交叉验证 + d_max sensitivity；(3) regime-dependent mapping；(4) **方法选择敏感性本身是主要贡献**（不再是\\\"独立贡献\\\"而是\\\"核心贡献\\\"）。\\n\\n## Anticipated T=20 下的表现\\n7. **ECE→perplexity \\\"反向因果\\\"已确认为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后 ECE→perplexity 0/4 SIG。之前的 post-hoc mechanistic hypothesis 无需讨论。论文只需报告差分前后对比\\n8. **TY 在 N=11 下完全不可用于区分真/伪关联** — d_max sensitivity 分析证实：d_max=0 过度宽松（连 effective_rank 都通过），d_max=1 过度保守（连 token_entropy→distinct_1 都拒绝），adaptive 因 ADF 不稳定而跨 seed 震荡。论文**不能**用 TY 作为\\\"正确答案\\\"。**spurious_rate 必须双版本报告**：含 TY 75%（16 pair 中 12 仅 TY 判伪）、排除 TY 后 62.5%（16 pair 中 10 在 4 种非 TY 方法下一致）。ECE→distinct_2/3 被 TY 判伪但在其余 4 种方法全部 4/4 SIG——这本身是\\\"方法选择改变分类\\\"的例证（Claim 3 素材）\\n9. **ECE 地位为 conditional secondary canary (D020)** — ECE 在 cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0）和 permutation Granger（差分后 4/4 SIG）下与 token_entropy 等效，ECE→distinct_2/3 在排除 TY 的 4 种方法中全部通过。\\\"secondary\\\" 标签**完全由 TY 的 Type II error 驱动**（TY 仅通过 ECE→distinct_1 3/4 seed）。论文标注：\\\"secondary only in TY consistency; equivalent across all other methods\\\"。plan_006（N=21）为最终判定——若 TY 在更大 N 下确认 ECE→distinct_2/3，升级为 co-primary\\n10. **d_max sensitivity 曲线是论文的 key figure** — 三组 d_max（0/adaptive/1）× spurious rate 的变化是 Claim 3 的核心可视化，展示\\\"方法选择如何改变结论\\\"\\n11. **统计效力必须显式论证** — Dream 三档一致 CRITICAL：T=10 下 TY 的 DOF 严重不足。论文必须包含 bootstrap/permutation 验证 + plan_006 (T=20) 结果。所有 p-value 必须经 BH FDR 校正\\n12. **\\\"phase diagram\\\" → \\\"regime map\\\"** — 除非 α 分辨率达到 ≥6 个非平凡值 + 提供 α* uncertainty interval，否则用 \\\"predictability regime map\\\" 或 \\\"regime-dependent analysis\\\"。4-5 个 α 值不足以支撑物理学意义上的 \\\"phase diagram\\\"\\n13. **Scheffer et al. 是必引文献** — critical slowing down / early-warning indicators 框架是本研究的知识祖先。论文 Related Work 必须引用并定位\\n14. **CRS 降级为 illustration** — 不列入核心 contribution。CRS 是显然的反应式干预，Dream 三档一致认为 trivial/confounded\\n15. **TY 正式降级为辅助指标 (D015)** — Bootstrap power analysis (N=1000 Monte Carlo): T=10 power=0.05-0.20, T=20 power=0.17, T=30 power=0.29 (d_max≥1)。核心分析框架改为 cross-correlation + permutation test + BH FDR 校正。TY 仅用于 Claim 3 展示（方法敏感性 + power analysis figure），不再作为 lead-lag 判定方法\\n16. **Perplexity 通道确认为伪回归 (D017)** — Permutation Granger 差分版：canary→perplexity 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99)，canary→diversity 差分后 12/12 SIG（6 pair × 4 seed 中，token_entropy 和 ECE 各 3 pair 全通过）。差分前后结论翻转（perplexity: 6-8/8→0/8; diversity: 12/12→12/12）是 Claim 3 的关键 figure 素材。论文 Method 节须注明：(1) T=11 first-difference 后 T=10，circular shift 仅 9 个唯一排列，null distribution 高度离散；(2) 尽管如此，diversity 通道 F 值远超任何排列值，结论不受离散性影响\\n17. **f² 效应量报告约束 (D019 更新)** — 差分后 f²=102-435（token_entropy→distinct_1），因 df_resid=5-6 任何真实信号都产生极大 f²（RSS_unrestricted 趋近零）。论文**禁止**用 Cohen 的 0.02/0.15/0.35 基准解读这些 f² 值。正确做法：permutation p-value 为主要推断证据，f² 仅作\\\"效应存在\\\"的定性指示，不做定量比较。**代码层面**：当 df_resid < 20 时，分析脚本输出 `f2_size=\\\"df_too_low\\\"` 而非 small/medium/large 分类（D019 T3 已实现于 permutation_granger.py）\\n18. **2×2 差分对比表是 Claim 3 核心 figure** — channel type（canary→diversity vs canary→perplexity）× differencing status（undifferenced vs differenced）的 2×2 表最紧凑展示\\\"一个方法论选择翻转整条通道的结论\\\"。diversity 通道差分前后均 SIG（F 不降反升），perplexity 通道差分后归零。比 d_max sensitivity 曲线更直观地展示差分的决定性影响\\n19. **method_ablation 必须双版本报告 (D019)** — spurious_rate 报告含 TY（5-method: 75%）和排除 TY（4-method: 62.5%）两个数字。ECE→distinct_2/3 在 TY 下被判伪但在其余 4 种方法中 4/4 SIG——TY 的 Type II error 驱动的分类差异本身是 Claim 3 素材。论文主文用 4-method 数字作主要结论，5-method 数字用于 Claim 3 方法敏感性讨论\\n20. **ECE canary 可靠性是 regime-dependent (D021)** — α=0.25 下 ECE gen 1 下降 -14%（反向信号），gen 2+ 才上升至 +77%。α=1.0 下 gen 1 直接 +2926%。ECE 作为早期预警 canary 仅在高 α（≥0.50，待 a050 确认）下可靠。低 α 下首代反向信号意味着 ECE 不能用于\\\"一代内预警\\\"。这丰富 Claim 2：canary 可靠性本身是 regime-dependent。D019 \\\"co-primary\\\" 限定为 \\\"high-α regime only\\\"\\n21. **Claim 1 α 适用范围确认收窄 (D022→D025)** — a050 结果确认 scenario (b)：α=0.50 两种因果检验方法 FDR 后归零（TY 0/8，permG 0/8），三种 correlation/rule-based 方法全部通过（naive 8/8，differenced 8/8，threshold 8/8）→ 3/5 方法通过，未达 ≥4/5 共识。α=0.25 也仅 TY 3/8 FDR-sig（power 存疑）。**当前 Granger-causal precedence 仅 α=1.0 稳固。** α=0.75（QUEUED）将决定 boundary：若 α=0.75 显著 → \\\"α ≥ 0.75\\\"；若也 null → \\\"α=1.0 only\\\"。论文措辞：Claim 1 主句限定为 \\\"at high contamination levels\\\"，Claim 2 regime map 展示 detectability boundary 的确切位置。不删 \\\"Granger-causal\\\"——差分后 α=1.0 仍 24/24 FDR-sig 是货真价实的 Granger 证据\\n22. **α=0.50 null 结果是 positive finding (D025)** — 中等污染下 canary 与 downstream 同步退化（temporal lead 消失），correlation/rule-based 方法（naive, differenced, threshold）100% sig vs 因果检验方法（permG, TY）0% sig 是 Claim 3（method × regime interaction）最极端的例证。论文呈现策略：(1) 此非单调 detectability 模式本身是 regime map 的核心特征；(2) **贡献定位是映射可预测性边界，不是声称通用预警**——α=1.0（100% 合成替换）在实践中极端，practical utility argument 须等 α=0.75 结果定调：若 α=0.75 有信号则 boundary 在 0.50-0.75，实用价值显著提升；若也归零则 utility 论述须谨慎，退回纯 \\\"boundary mapping\\\" 贡献定位；(3) α=0.50 null 不是方法失败，是 signal-to-noise 的物理边界\\n23. **α-sweep 全部 single seed 是当前最大弱点 (D026)** — 非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 -40.3% vs α=0.50 -37.3%）和 ECE regime switch 假说均基于 seed=42 的 point estimate，无置信区间。论文中非单调性结论**必须标注 \\\"observed at seed=42; multi-seed confirmation required\\\"**。multi-seed（α=0.25/0.50 各 2-3 seeds）是从 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要条件，优先于 finer α grid（a010/a060）。非单调性在主文仅报告现象（配 drift 表格），\\\"entropy floor\\\" 机制假说放 Discussion\\n24. **α=0.25 双向 Granger 不改变 Claim 1 叙事 (D026)** — d1→entropy raw p=0.023（FDR 后 0.093 不显著），但效应量仅 f²=1.30 vs entropy→d1 的 f²=27.21（21 倍差异）。强单向因果 + 弱反馈，不是对称 coupled dynamics。α=1.0 反方向不显著，α=0.50 反方向 p=0.224。论文可加一句：\\\"marginally significant feedback at mild contamination (f²=1.30, 21× weaker than primary direction)\\\" 但不需要改写核心叙事\\n25. **证据强度排序：Claim 3 > Claim 2 > Claim 1 (D026)** — Reviewer 独立评估。Claim 3（method sensitivity）最强且 novel、well-supported；Claim 2（regime-dependent）次之（但需 multi-seed）；Claim 1（Granger-causal）scope 最窄（仅 α=1.0）。论文 framing 应确保 Claim 3 的方法论贡献获得足够篇幅，不因 Claim 1 的 \\\"canary prediction\\\" 叙事而被矮化\\n\", \"total_chars\": 15252, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": - Claim 1: Canary→Diversity Granger-causal precedence (case study)
- Claim 2: Regime-Dependent Predictability
- Claim 3: Method Selection Sensitivity (headline)


Looking at the Phase 3 dynamics from seed s42, I see a three-stage pattern: rapid collapse in Phase 1, stabilization in Phase 2, then a secondary decline in Phase 3. The critical issue is that this comes from a single seed with non-standard parameters—full WikiText-103 and 10k steps per generation rather than the 5k used elsewhere—so I need to be cautious about how much weight to give it relative to the broader experimental design.

The evidence really isn't strong enough to elevate this to a core claim. It's only one seed, only one α value, and the Phase 3 declines are modest compared to the initial collapse. Promoting this based on such limited data would be premature.

More importantly, Phase 3 dynamics don't align with the paper's main focus on method sensitivity and the consensus framework. It's an interesting side observation about the phenomenon itself, but not central to the methodology story. And if s43 fails to reproduce it, claiming multi-phase collapse as a core contribution could seriously damage the paper's credibility.

The right move is to mention this in the discussion as a secondary finding with a clear caveat about the single-seed limitation. If s43 confirms it, I could include it as a supplementary observation in the main text, but it shouldn't be elevated to core claim status.

So the narrative structure holds: Claim 3 anchors on method sensitivity with the ablation data, Claim 2 uses the regime map across different α values, and Claim 1 is the MVP case study. Phase 3 serves as supporting evidence that longer observation windows matter, which I can note briefly in the discussion.

The risk profile is actually quite favorable—Phase 3 isn't load-bearing for any of the core claims, so whether it reproduces or not doesn't jeopardize the paper's main contributions. If it does show up in the data, I mention it; if not, it's either omitted or relegated to a brief note about single-run observations. Either way, the three central claims remain solid.

For the framing document, I'm thinking I shouldn't elevate Phase 3 to core claim status, but I should flag that it's single-seed evidence requiring replication and note where it would fit if confirmed—probably as a discussion point about observation windows rather than a main result. The temporal phase dynamics are actually complementary to the regime-dependent picture from Claim 2, but adding that axis on weak evidence would dilute the narrative, so I'm keeping the focus on what's already well-supported.

The director's suggestion to upgrade from "canary predicts collapse" to "canary reveals multi-phase dynamics" is actually pointing backward—the current framing already moved away from phenomenon-focused claims toward methodology, which was the whole point of D027's constraints. So this would be a regression, not an upgrade.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me verify the Phase 3 metrics independently:

**Phase 2 plateau (gen 5-12):**
- d2: gen5=0.3867 → gen12=0.3859 → Δ = -0.0008 (-0.2%)
- d3: gen5=0.6358 → gen12=0.6339 → Δ = -0.0019 (-0.3%)
- d1: gen5=0.078 → gen12=0.0779 → Δ ≈ 0 (-0.1%)


The plateau holds up—barely any movement across those seven generations. Now looking at the secondary decline from generation 12 to 17, I'm seeing much sharper drops: d2 falls 6.3%, d3 drops 7.5%, and even d1 declines by 3%.

But wait, the registry shows different percentages—d2 at -5.8% and d3 at -6.9%. Let me recalculate to see where the discrepancy is. Ah, I think the registry might be using generation 13 as the starting point instead of 12. Checking that range: d2 from gen 13 to 17 gives exactly -5.8%, and d3 matches at -6.9%. That's the alignment.

So the Phase 3 boundary at gen 13 is somewhat arbitrary—the decline was already underway at gen 12, not a sharp cutoff. And interestingly, token entropy has been fluctuating slightly across these generations rather than showing a clear trend.

Looking at the full picture across Phase 3: diversity metrics resume their decline, entropy ticks up a bit, calibration stays essentially flat, and perplexity keeps climbing steeply. This doesn't fundamentally change the core narrative—Phase 3 is still characterized by continued degradation in some dimensions while others plateau or stabilize.

The key insight is that this single experimental run with one seed and one α value is exactly the kind of limited evidence the paper itself warns against over-interpreting. Plus, the experimental setup differs meaningfully from the T=10 work—longer training on a larger dataset could produce different dynamics. Since the paper's main contribution is methodological rather than phenomenological, adding Phase 3 observations would dilute that focus. There's also a deeper methodological tension here: the canary-to-diversity lead that holds at T=10 breaks down in Phase 3, where token entropy actually increases while diversity decreases, which complicates the clean narrative.

Pushing back on the suggestion to reframe this as "multi-phase collapse dynamics"—that's actually moving backward. The paper already stepped away from "canary predicts collapse," and the core contribution is about methodology, not describing new phenomena. The current structure already optimizes the findings: Claim 3 gets the method sensitivity analysis, Claim 2 gets the regime mapping across multiple α values, and Claim 1 has the case study. Phase 3 works best as a brief Discussion note about what longer observation windows reveal, rather than upgrading it to a core claim.

There's a tricky detail though: in Phase 3, token entropy actually rises slightly while diversity declines, which contradicts the "entropy leads diversity decline" narrative if I'm not careful. That needs to be handled explicitly. And if s43 doesn't reproduce Phase 3, the risk is minimal—it's not part of any core claim, so I can either remove it or mention it as a single-seed observation without weakening the paper.

For paper_framing.md, I should make only minor updates: note Phase 3 as discussion material rather than a core contribution, flag the entropy-diversity divergence as something needing explanation, and document how the observation window affects conclusions. But I won't change the core claims, narrative structure, or elevate Phase 3 to a main contribution.
```

Now I'm recalculating the performance metrics from the raw data to verify the α=0.75 results, and I'm seeing a consistent non-monotonic pattern across all dimensions—the diversity drift at α=0.75 falls between the α=0.50 and α=1.0 values, which is interesting because it suggests the relationship isn't simply linear with the alpha parameter. The peak diversity loss at α=0.25 followed by monotonic improvement toward α=1.0 might actually reflect a collapse artifact — at α=1.0, the model's entropy drops so severely that it just repeats the same tokens, which artificially constrains diversity metrics, whereas lower α values generate more varied (though degraded) text that shows larger diversity drops. For entropy and ECE, I'm seeing the expected monotonic patterns: entropy worsens progressively with higher α, while calibration error explodes dramatically at α=1.0.

Perplexity follows the same trend, jumping from +21.1% at α=0.50 to over 3600% at α=1.0. Looking at the generation-by-generation breakdown for the canary→diversity lead, entropy collapses sharply in the first generation but diversity metrics barely budge, then in the second generation entropy stabilizes while diversity metrics drop significantly — suggesting the model's initial response is to lose confidence rather than immediately change its output distribution.

ECE shows a similar pattern with a dramatic spike in generation 1 before settling down. As the model continues training, entropy plateaus around generation 5-10 while diversity keeps declining gradually, and ECE stabilizes in the 0.19-0.21 range, though perplexity continues its slow climb.

Now I'm considering whether the temporal lead pattern I'm seeing in the raw metrics will hold up under formal Granger causality testing at α=0.75. The effect sizes are noticeably larger here compared to the α=0.50 case, which could give me more statistical power, but with only 11 time points and a single seed, I'm skeptical that FDR-corrected significance will emerge. This is really the crux of the decision—whether Granger tests show significance will determine how broadly I can claim the entropy-diversity precedence relationship.

The cross-alpha analysis with the new α=0.75 data still needs to run. Meanwhile, the GPU situation has shifted: cuda:0 is now heavily loaded with the GPT-2-medium training (55GB memory usage), indicating the experiment has moved into the actual training phase, while cuda:1 is idle after the a075 run completed.

I can also see the canary temporal lead effect in the raw data—entropy drops sharply in the first generation (-32.3%), but diversity doesn't follow until the next generation (-15%+), suggesting entropy is the leading indicator.

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — alpha_sweep_a075_s42\n\n**结论**: WARNING（需要立即跟进动作）\n\n### 发现\n\n**1. 实验完整性** [severity: info]\n\n实验正常完成，EXIT_CODE=0。gen_0 至 gen_10 全部产出，all_metrics.json 完整（11 generation × 10 指标）。\n\n配置核对：seed=42, α=0.75, Pythia-410m (default), wikitext103_5k, 5k steps/gen, cuda:1 — 与 spec command 一致。\n\n**2. 非单调 dose-response 模式确认** [severity: info]\n\nα=0.75 的 diversity drift 填入后，5-point 趋势清晰：\n\n| Metric | α=0.00 | α=0.25 | α=0.50 | α=0.75 | α=1.00 |\n|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|\n| d1 | -13.8% | **-40.3%** | -37.3% | -32.7% | -28.3% |\n| d2 | -9.8% | **-57.1%** | -51.5% | -41.3% | -35.2% |\n| d3 | -4.5% | **-59.9%** | -52.4% | -38.3% | -31.6% |\n| entropy | -5.1% | -4.1% | -18.6% | -27.7% | -60.5% |\n| ECE | +51.7% | +79.7% | +843% | +1810% | +3763% |\n| ppl | -21.7% | -17.2% | +21.1% | +182% | +3625% |\n\nDiversity loss 在 α=0.25 达到峰值后随 α 递减（非单调），而 entropy/ECE/ppl 随 α 单调增大。这个分离值得论文讨论：高 α 下模型 entropy 崩塌过快，生成高度重复文本，反而使 distinct_n \"地板化\"速度减缓（文本坍缩到少量 token 模式时，distinct_n 的下降空间受限）。这一机制假说需要在论文中谨慎表述——当前仍为单 seed 观察（constraint #23 适用）。\n\n**3. Canary temporal lead 在原始数据中可见** [severity: info]\n\nGen-by-gen 检查：\n- Gen 0→1: entropy -32.3% (3.234→2.191), d1 -0.7%, d2 -0.7% → canary 先动\n- Gen 1→2: entropy -4.1%, d1 -15.2%, d2 -15.4% → diversity 跟进\n- ECE gen 0→1: +1130% (0.011→0.133) → ECE 也领先 1 gen\n\n时序 lead 模式与 α=1.0 定性一致。但定量关键是 permutation Granger FDR 是否通过——这决定 ROADMAP 决策树走向。\n\n**4. Claim 1 scope 决策节点** [severity: warning]\n\nROADMAP 定义的决策树：\n- Granger FDR-sig at α=0.75 → Claim 1 scope = \"α ≥ 0.75\"\n- Granger null → Claim 1 = \"α=1.0 only\"\n\nα=0.75 的 entropy drift (-27.7%) 大于 α=0.50 (-18.6%)，信号更强，但仍远小于 α=1.0 (-60.5%)。参考 α=0.50 结果（permG 0/4 FDR-sig, raw p 0.019-0.095），α=0.75 的 raw p 可能更小但 T=11 单 seed FDR 校正后能否通过存在不确定性。\n\n**Agent 必须立即运行 cross-alpha 5-point 分析**（加入 α=0.75 数据），这是 Claim 1 论文措辞的硬依赖。\n\n**5. cuda:1 空闲** [severity: warning]\n\na075 完成后 cuda:1 已释放（nvidia-smi: 0 MiB, 0% util）。按 ROADMAP，下一步应提交 alpha_sweep_a025_s43（D026 multi-seed）。cuda:1 每小时空闲都是浪费。\n\n**6.
